#! Emerald Champions hand-authored campaign teams (see scripts/emerald_champions_teams.py)

# ---- E0001 Route103 cap=14 Opening rival battle
## E0001 TRAINER_BRENDAN_ROUTE_103_MUDKIP class=rival
# Rival / single
plan: The rival's species is resolved from the player's starter generation and choice. Every branch is level 13 with a Life Orb, perfect preparation, its legal Pledge STAB, Protect, and two species-specific current moves: Treecko and Torchic can threaten setup while Mudkip commits to immediate physical pressure.
crack: This is a one-on-one with no hidden reserve. Focus Sash, Eviolite, Leftovers, priority, status, setup denial, Counter or Mirror Coat, Endeavor, recovery, and direct coverage all provide different solutions across the starter families.
TREECKO @LIFE_ORB OVERGROW JOLLY PS -1 | GRASS_PLEDGE, FACADE, SWORDS_DANCE, QUICK_ATTACK
## E0001 TRAINER_BRENDAN_ROUTE_103_TORCHIC class=rival
# Rival / single
MUDKIP @LIFE_ORB TORRENT ADAMANT PS -1 | WATER_PLEDGE, DOUBLE_EDGE, WATERFALL, ROCK_SLIDE

# ---- E0002 Route102 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0002 TRAINER_CALVIN_1 class=regular
plan: Calvin's Poochyena Howls for both partners while Zigzagoon Belly Drums into Extreme Speed; Guts Taillow finishes anything the Drum misses.
crack: Protect or Fake Out the Drum turn, hit Zigzagoon at half HP with anything, and answer Taillow with Rock or Electric; Intimidate ruins the whole plan.
POOCHYENA @FOCUS_SASH RATTLED JOLLY PS -2 | CRUNCH, PLAY_ROUGH, SUCKER_PUNCH, HOWL
ZIGZAGOON @SITRUS_BERRY GLUTTONY ADAMANT PS -1 | BELLY_DRUM, EXTREME_SPEED, SEED_BOMB, PROTECT
TAILLOW @TOXIC_ORB GUTS JOLLY PS 0 | FACADE, BRAVE_BIRD, QUICK_ATTACK, PROTECT
# ---- E0003 Route102 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0003 TRAINER_RICK class=regular
plan: Spinarak lays Sticky Web and Toxic Thread, then Sturdy Dwebble and No Guard Karrablast outspeed webbed targets and trade Megahorns.
crack: Flying and Levitate ignore the Web, Fire and Rock hit every bug, and Rapid Spin or Defog erases the setup turn Spinarak spent.
SPINARAK @FOCUS_SASH INSOMNIA JOLLY FS -2 | STICKY_WEB, TOXIC_THREAD, POISON_JAB, SUCKER_PUNCH
DWEBBLE @EVIOLITE STURDY ADAMANT PB -1 | ROCK_SLIDE, X_SCISSOR, KNOCK_OFF, PROTECT
KARRABLAST @LIFE_ORB NO_GUARD ADAMANT PS 0 | MEGAHORN, DRILL_RUN, KNOCK_OFF, PROTECT
# ---- E0004 Route102 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0004 TRAINER_ALLEN class=regular
plan: Light Ball Pikachu Fake Outs while Paras Spores, buying the Choice Band Slakoth free Truant turns to swing Hammer Arm and Body Slam.
crack: Grass types, Overcoat and Safety Goggles laugh at Spore, Ground answers Pikachu, and Protect on Slakoth's active turn wastes half its life.
PIKACHU @LIGHT_BALL LIGHTNING_ROD JOLLY PS 0 | FAKE_OUT, VOLT_TACKLE, ELECTROWEB, PROTECT
PARAS @EVIOLITE DRY_SKIN ADAMANT PB -1 | SPORE, X_SCISSOR, LEECH_LIFE, PROTECT
SLAKOTH @CHOICE_BAND TRUANT ADAMANT PB -1 | BODY_SLAM, HAMMER_ARM, NIGHT_SLASH, ROCK_SLIDE
# ---- E0005 Route102 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0005 TRAINER_TIANA class=casual
plan: Skitty Fake Outs and Sings so Swirlix can eat its Sitrus Berry, Unburden into double speed and fire Draining Kiss and coverage.
crack: Steel or Poison walls Swirlix, Taunt stops Skitty's Sing, and any priority move erases Swirlix once it is in Sitrus range.
SKITTY @FOCUS_SASH WONDER_SKIN JOLLY FS -3 | FAKE_OUT, SING, HELPING_HAND, PROTECT
SWIRLIX @SITRUS_BERRY UNBURDEN MODEST SS -2 | DRAINING_KISS, ENERGY_BALL, THUNDERBOLT, PROTECT
# ---- E0006 Route104 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0006 TRAINER_BILLY class=regular

crack: Electric and Rock crush the rain pair; Ditto copies a Pokemon with Struggle-bait PP or one that is weak to its own STAB.
LOTAD @EVIOLITE SWIFT_SWIM MODEST SS -1 | RAIN_DANCE, MUDDY_WATER, GIGA_DRAIN, ICE_BEAM
